import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Deriv
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Pow.Real
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue

/-! # Salt timing for 1 L of water at 20 C: when should NaCl be added? (cycle 6)

## REVIEW PACKET
SUPPLIED BY THE USER: 1 L water at 20 C in a pot on a stove; salt will be added; goal = salted water reaches
its boiling point soonest; salt amount, pot, heater, room unspecified.

ACCEPTED EARLIER (kept): lumped Newtonian heating dH/dt = P - h (T - 20), constant stove power P, no evaporation,
salt (crystal at 20 C) dissolves instantly at the moment of addition (total enthalpy conserved).

MODEL CHOICES / ASSUMPTIONS (all appear as `Premises` fields or theorem hypotheses):
 * NaCl; r = salt mass / water mass in [1/50, 1/20] (20-50 g in the 0.998 kg of water of 1 L); mass fraction
   w = r/(1+r) (conversion proved in `w_range`), n = 1000*0.998*r/58.44 mol (`nSalt`, bounds in `n_range`).
 * Pot+water enthalpy Hw(T) has heat capacity Cw(T) >= 4100 J/K on 10..101 C (0.998 kg water >= 4.17 kJ/K; pot adds).
 * Apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl in the final solution = Laliberte (2009) correlation `PhiC`
   (empirical coefficients a1..a6 from Laliberte, J. Chem. Eng. Data 54, 1725; fit range 1.5-120 C, w <= 0.26).
   Ph(T) is the apparent molar enthalpy of NaCl in that solution: dPh/dT = PhiC (definition of Phi_C).
   Solution enthalpy = (water at T) + n*Ph(T)   (definition of apparent molar enthalpy; `solEnthalpy`).
 * Crystal NaCl enthalpy Hcr with Cp_cr in [40,100] J/K/mol on 20..25 C (Archer 1992 Part I, Eqs 4-6: Cp(298.15)=50.16;
   my evaluation of the spline gives 50.04..50.16 on 20..25 C).
 * Parker NSRDS-NBS 2 (1965): Delta H_inf(25 C, NaCl(c) -> infinite dilution) = 928 +- 5 cal/mol (printed p.26);
   Phi_L(25 C, m) (Table X, p.50) is RELATIVE to infinite dilution: Ph(25) - Hcr(25) = Hinf + Phi_L.
   For the regime m = 17.1 r in [0.34, 0.86] mol/kg Table X gives Phi_L = 83, 49, 20, -41 cal/mol at m = .2775,
   .5551, .7401, 1.1101 and dPhi_L/dm^(1/2) < 0 for m >= .185, so Phi_L in [-41, 88]; premise used: [-41, 100] cal. cal = 4.184 J.
 * Boiling temperature Tb of the final solution: 20 <= Tb <= 101 C (1 atm, <= 4.8 wt%: ideal elevation <= ~0.9 K); stove
   beats losses at Tb; Tw, Ts = temperature as function of total enthalpy (inverse of Hw, Hs; existence by IVT,
   `inverse_exists`); tau_w, tau_s = elapsed time as function of enthalpy: d tau/dH = 1/(P - h (T(H) - 20)).

PROVED (not assumed): Phi_C < 0 and >= -105 J/(K mol) for all regime/temperatures (concavity + exp bounds, `cs_sign`);
 trapezoid enclosure Ph(101) - Ph(20) >= -3363 J/mol from 11 Taylor-bounded exp evaluations (`ph_drop`);
 dissolution at 20 C >= 3890 J/mol via Kirchhoff (`thermo_facts`); salt addition at any x in [20,Tb] cools
 (Hs(x) - Hw(x) >= 0), solution enthalpy strictly increasing, mixing at 20 C stays above 10 C; and the timing
 comparison (`timing_core`, `answer_theorem`).
CONCLUSIONS: `cs_sign`, `answer_theorem` (salting at the start is no slower than salting at any later water temperature).
-/


/-! ### calculus helpers -/
lemma dmono (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, 0 ≤ f T) : MonotoneOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T (interior_subset hT)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma danti (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, f T ≤ 0) : AntitoneOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T (interior_subset hT)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma dstrict (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, 0 < f T) : StrictMonoOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply strictMonoOn_of_deriv_pos (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma lin_deriv (F : ℝ → ℝ) (d c T : ℝ) (h : HasDerivAt F d T) :
    HasDerivAt (fun y => F y + c*y) (d + c) T :=
  (h.add ((hasDerivAt_id T).const_mul c)).congr_deriv (by simp)

/-- Taylor-polynomial upper bound for exp on [0,4] (from `Real.exp_bound`, 7 terms, applied to x/4 and raised to the 4th power) -/
noncomputable def Q (x : ℝ) : ℝ := ((∑ m ∈ Finset.range 7, (x/4)^m / (m.factorial:ℝ)) + (x/4)^7 * (8/35280))^4

lemma exp_ub (x : ℝ) (h0 : 0 ≤ x) (h4 : x ≤ 4) : Real.exp x ≤ Q x := by
  have hy : |x/4| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
  have hb := Real.exp_bound hy (n := 7) (by norm_num)
  rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ x/4)] at hb
  have h1 := (abs_le.mp hb).2
  have e : ((Nat.succ 7 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((Nat.factorial 7 : ℝ) * (7:ℕ)) = 8/35280 := by norm_num [Nat.factorial]
  rw [e] at h1
  have e2 : Real.exp x = Real.exp (x/4) ^ 4 := by
    rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
  rw [e2]; exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (Real.exp_pos _).le (by linarith) 4

/-! ### Laliberté (2009) NaCl correlation: empirical inputs (exposed) and derived consequences -/
noncomputable def Mnacl : ℝ := 58.44
noncomputable def a1 : ℝ := -0.0693559668993322
noncomputable def a2 : ℝ := -0.0782134167486952
noncomputable def a3 : ℝ := 3.84798479408635
noncomputable def a4 : ℝ := -11.2762109247072
noncomputable def a5 : ℝ := 8.73187698542672
noncomputable def a6 : ℝ := 1.81245930472755

/-- apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl in aqueous solution, J/(K mol): Mnacl * (Laliberté solute heat capacity
    Cp_i in J/(g K)); w = salt mass fraction, T in C -/
noncomputable def PhiC (w T : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  Mnacl * (a1 * Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*w) + a5 * w ^ a6)

/-- Laliberté's mixing rule Cp_sol = w_w Cp_w + w_s Cp_i gives C_solution - C_water = ms*Cp_i = (ms/M)*(M*Cp_i):
    per mole of salt the heat-capacity change is M*Cp_i = PhiC. -/
lemma apparent_capacity (ms mw M cpw cpi : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (hs : 0 < ms + mw) :
    (ms+mw) * ((mw/(ms+mw)) * cpw + (ms/(ms+mw)) * cpi) - mw * cpw = (ms/M) * (M * cpi) := by
  field_simp; ring

lemma phi_mono_w (w T : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w) : PhiC (1/51) T ≤ PhiC w T := by
  unfold PhiC
  have h1 : Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*w) ≤ Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*(1/51)) :=
    Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by unfold a4; linarith)
  have h2 : (1/51:ℝ) ^ a6 ≤ w ^ a6 := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by norm_num) h (by unfold a6; norm_num)
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have ha5 : 0 < a5 := by unfold a5; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by nlinarith) hM.le

lemma phi_neg (w T : ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w) (h2 : w ≤ 1/21) (t1 : 10 ≤ T) (t2 : T ≤ 101) : PhiC w T < 0 := by
  unfold PhiC
  have hw : 0 < w := by linarith
  have e1 : w ^ a6 ≤ w ^ ((3:ℝ)/2) :=
    Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge hw (by linarith) (by unfold a6; norm_num)
  have e2 : w ^ ((3:ℝ)/2) = w * Real.sqrt w := by
    rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, show ((3:ℝ)/2) = 1 + 1/2 by norm_num, Real.rpow_add hw, Real.rpow_one]
  have e3 : Real.sqrt w ≤ 1/4 := by rw [Real.sqrt_le_left (by norm_num)]; nlinarith
  have e4 : w ^ a6 ≤ 1/84 := by have := Real.sqrt_nonneg w; nlinarith
  have q := Real.quadratic_le_exp_of_nonneg (x := 0.01*T) (by linarith)
  set E := Real.exp (0.01*T)
  have hα : 1.2 ≤ a2*T + a3*E + a4*w := by
    unfold a2 a3 a4
    nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 101 - T) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - 10)]
  have hex : 2 ≤ Real.exp (a2*T + a3*E + a4*w) := by linarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (a2*T + a3*E + a4*w)]
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  refine mul_neg_of_pos_of_neg hM ?_
  unfold a1 a5 at *
  nlinarith

/-- Φ_C is concave in T: its value at T lies above the chord between any a < b. -/
lemma phi_chord (w a b T : ℝ) (hab : a < b) (h1 : a ≤ T) (h2 : T ≤ b) :
    (b-a) * PhiC w T ≥ (b-T) * PhiC w a + (T-a) * PhiC w b := by
  set α : ℝ → ℝ := fun s => a2*s + a3*Real.exp (0.01*s) + a4*w with hαdef
  have ha3 : 0 < a3 := by unfold a3; norm_num
  have tang : ∀ s, Real.exp (α s) ≥ Real.exp (α T) * (1 + (a2 + a3*0.01*Real.exp (0.01*T)) * (s - T)) := by
    intro s
    have e1 : Real.exp (0.01*s) ≥ Real.exp (0.01*T) * (1 + 0.01*(s-T)) := by
      have : Real.exp (0.01*s) = Real.exp (0.01*T) * Real.exp (0.01*(s-T)) := by
        rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
      rw [this]
      nlinarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (0.01*(s-T)), Real.exp_pos (0.01*T)]
    have e2 : α s - α T ≥ (a2 + a3*0.01*Real.exp (0.01*T)) * (s - T) := by
      simp only [hαdef]; nlinarith
    have e3 : Real.exp (α s) = Real.exp (α T) * Real.exp (α s - α T) := by
      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
    rw [e3]
    nlinarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (α s - α T), Real.exp_pos (α T)]
  have ta := tang a
  have tb := tang b
  have hg : (b-a) * Real.exp (α T) ≤ (b-T) * Real.exp (α a) + (T-a) * Real.exp (α b) := by
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ta (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ b - T),
               mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left tb (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - a)]
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  unfold PhiC
  have : (b-a) * (a1 * Real.exp (α T)) ≥ (b-T) * (a1 * Real.exp (α a)) + (T-a) * (a1 * Real.exp (α b)) := by
    nlinarith
  simp only [hαdef] at this
  nlinarith [mul_pos hM (by linarith : (0:ℝ) < b - a)]

/-- trapezoid lower bound: if F' = f lies above its chord on [a,b] then F b - F a ≥ (b-a)(f a + f b)/2 -/
lemma trap (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a < b)
    (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hc : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, (b-a) * f T ≥ (b-T) * f a + (T-a) * f b) :
    (b-a)*(f a + f b)/2 ≤ F b - F a := by
  have hba : 0 < b - a := by linarith
  have hd : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt
      (fun T => F T - F a - (f a * (T-a) + (T-a)^2 * (f b - f a) / (2*(b-a)))) (f T - ((b-T) * f a + (T-a) * f b)/(b-a)) T := by
    intro T hT
    have u : HasDerivAt (fun T : ℝ => T - a) 1 T := (hasDerivAt_id T).sub_const a
    have v := (u.const_mul (f a)).add (((u.pow 2).mul_const (f b - f a)).div_const (2*(b-a)))
    convert (hF T hT).sub_const (F a) |>.sub v using 1
    field_simp; ring
  have := dmono _ _ a b hd (fun T hT => by
    have := hc T hT
    rw [sub_nonneg, div_le_iff₀ hba]; linarith) ⟨le_rfl, hab.le⟩ ⟨hab.le, le_rfl⟩ hab.le
  simp only at this
  have e : (f a * (b-a) + (b-a)^2 * (f b - f a) / (2*(b-a))) = (b-a)*(f a + f b)/2 := by
    field_simp; ring
  simp at this
  nlinarith [e]

lemma node (w T V β U L : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w)
    (hh : 0 ≤ T ∧ T ≤ 400 ∧ Q (0.01*T) ≤ V ∧ a2*T + a3*V + a4*(1/51) ≤ β ∧ 0 ≤ β ∧ β ≤ 4 ∧ Q β ≤ U ∧
      -L ≤ Mnacl*a1*U) : -L ≤ PhiC w T := by
  obtain ⟨h0, h4, hV, hb, hb0, hb4, hU, hL⟩ := hh
  refine le_trans ?_ (phi_mono_w w T h)
  have e1 : Real.exp (0.01*T) ≤ V := (exp_ub _ (by linarith) (by linarith)).trans hV
  have ha3 : 0 < a3 := by unfold a3; norm_num
  have e2 : Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*(1/51)) ≤ U :=
    (Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e1 ha3.le])).trans ((exp_ub β hb0 hb4).trans hU)
  have h5 : 0 ≤ a5 * (1/51:ℝ)^a6 := mul_nonneg (by unfold a5; norm_num) (Real.rpow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  unfold PhiC
  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e2 (mul_pos hM (neg_pos.mpr ha1)).le, mul_nonneg hM.le h5]

lemma nodes (w : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w) : -(104.51:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 10 ∧ -(74.77:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 20 ∧ -(56.07:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 30 ∧ -(44.29:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 40 ∧ -(37.06:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 50 ∧ -(33.04:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 60 ∧ -(31.6:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 70 ∧ -(32.65:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 80 ∧ -(36.75:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 90 ∧ -(45.49:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 100 ∧ -(46.73:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 101 :=
  ⟨node w 10 1.10519 3.2497 25.784 104.51 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 20 1.22142 2.9148 18.447 74.77 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 30 1.34987 2.6269 13.832 56.07 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 40 1.49184 2.3911 10.927 44.29 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 50 1.64874 2.2127 9.142 37.06 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 60 1.82213 2.0978 8.15 33.04 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 70 2.01377 2.0531 7.794 31.6 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 80 2.22556 2.0859 8.053 32.65 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 90 2.45962 2.2044 9.066 36.75 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 100 2.7183 2.4177 11.222 45.49 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 101 2.74562 2.4446 11.527 46.73 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1])⟩

lemma piece (w : ℝ) (Ph : ℝ → ℝ) (a b La Lb : ℝ) (hab : a < b) (ha : 10 ≤ a) (hb : b ≤ 101)
    (hPh : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC w T) T)
    (hA : -La ≤ PhiC w a) (hB : -Lb ≤ PhiC w b) : -((b-a)*(La+Lb)/2) ≤ Ph b - Ph a := by
  have := trap Ph (PhiC w) a b hab (fun T hT => hPh T ⟨by linarith [hT.1], by linarith [hT.2]⟩)
    (fun T hT => phi_chord w a b T hab hT.1 hT.2)
  nlinarith

lemma phi_floor (w T : ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w) (t1 : 10 ≤ T) (t2 : T ≤ 101) : -105 ≤ PhiC w T := by
  have c := phi_chord w 10 101 T (by norm_num) t1 t2
  obtain ⟨n10, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, n101⟩ := nodes w h1
  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 101 - T) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 105 - 104.51),
             mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - 10) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 46.73 - 0)]

/-- Total change of apparent molar enthalpy of NaCl from 20 C to 101 C is at least -3363 J/mol. -/
lemma ph_drop (w : ℝ) (Ph : ℝ → ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w)
    (hPh : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC w T) T) : -3363 ≤ Ph 101 - Ph 20 := by
  obtain ⟨n10, n20, n30, n40, n50, n60, n70, n80, n90, n100, n101⟩ := nodes w h1
  have p0 := piece w Ph 20 30 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n20 n30
  have p1 := piece w Ph 30 40 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n30 n40
  have p2 := piece w Ph 40 50 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n40 n50
  have p3 := piece w Ph 50 60 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n50 n60
  have p4 := piece w Ph 60 70 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n60 n70
  have p5 := piece w Ph 70 80 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n70 n80
  have p6 := piece w Ph 80 90 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n80 n90
  have p7 := piece w Ph 90 100 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n90 n100
  have p8 := piece w Ph 100 101 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n100 n101
  norm_num at *
  linarith

/-! ## Heating-time model (accepted Newtonian lumped model; state variable = total enthalpy H) -/

/-- Time to reach the boiling point when salt is added while the pure water is at Ta: pure-water branch up to
    total enthalpy Hw Ta, then solution branch from the same enthalpy up to Hs Tb.
    τw, τs = elapsed time as a function of total enthalpy on the two branches. -/
def boilTime (τw τs Hw Hs : ℝ → ℝ) (T0 Tb Ta : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (τw (Hw Ta) - τw (Hw T0)) + (τs (Hs Tb) - τs (Hw Ta))

theorem timing_core (P h Tr Tl Tb : ℝ) (Hw Hs Tw Ts τw τs : ℝ → ℝ)
    (hh : 0 ≤ h) (hP : h*(Tb-Tr) < P) (hl : Tl ≤ Tr) (hr : Tr ≤ Tb)
    (hHw : StrictMonoOn Hw (Set.Icc Tl Tb)) (hHs : StrictMonoOn Hs (Set.Icc Tl Tb))
    (hTw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Tb), Tw H ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb ∧ Hw (Tw H) = H)
    (hTs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hs Tl) (Hs Tb), Ts H ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb ∧ Hs (Ts H) = H)
    (hτw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Tb), HasDerivAt τw (1/(P - h*(Tw H - Tr))) H)
    (hτs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hs Tl) (Hs Tb), HasDerivAt τs (1/(P - h*(Ts H - Tr))) H)
    (hcool : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc Tr Tb, Hw x ≤ Hs x) (hlow : Hs Tl ≤ Hw Tr) :
    ∀ Ta ∈ Set.Icc Tr Tb, boilTime τw τs Hw Hs Tr Tb Tr ≤ boilTime τw τs Hw Hs Tr Tb Ta := by
  intro Ta hTa
  have hTrI : Tr ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl, hr⟩
  have hTaI : Ta ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl.trans hTa.1, hTa.2⟩
  have hTbI : Tb ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl.trans hr, le_rfl⟩
  have w1 : Hw Tr ≤ Hw Ta := hHw.monotoneOn hTrI hTaI hTa.1
  have w2 : Hw Ta ≤ Hw Tb := hHw.monotoneOn hTaI hTbI hTa.2
  have s1 : Hw Tb ≤ Hs Tb := hcool Tb ⟨hr, le_rfl⟩
  have hpos : ∀ x, x ≤ Tb → 0 < P - h*(x - Tr) := by
    intro x hx
    have : h*(x-Tr) ≤ h*(Tb-Tr) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hh
    linarith
  have key : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Ta), 1/(P - h*(Tw H - Tr)) - 1/(P - h*(Ts H - Tr)) ≥ 0 := by
    intro H hH
    obtain ⟨hx, hxH⟩ := hTw H ⟨hH.1, hH.2.trans w2⟩
    obtain ⟨hy, hyH⟩ := hTs H ⟨hlow.trans hH.1, hH.2.trans (w2.trans s1)⟩
    have hxr : Tr ≤ Tw H := by
      by_contra hc
      have := hHw hx hTrI (not_le.mp hc)
      rw [hxH] at this
      linarith [hH.1]
    have hc := hcool _ ⟨hxr, hx.2⟩
    rw [hxH] at hc
    have hyx : Ts H ≤ Tw H := by
      by_contra hcon
      have := hHs hx hy (not_le.mp hcon)
      rw [hyH] at this
      linarith
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith : Ts H - Tr ≤ Tw H - Tr) hh
    have := one_div_le_one_div_of_le (hpos _ hx.2) (by linarith : P - h*(Tw H - Tr) ≤ P - h*(Ts H - Tr))
    linarith
  have mono := dmono (fun H => τw H - τs H) _ (Hw Tr) (Hw Ta)
    (fun H hH => (hτw H ⟨hH.1, hH.2.trans w2⟩).sub (hτs H ⟨hlow.trans hH.1, hH.2.trans (w2.trans s1)⟩)) key
  have := mono ⟨le_rfl, w1⟩ ⟨w1, le_rfl⟩ w1
  simp only [boilTime] at this ⊢
  linarith

/-- sanity: the inverse temperature functions Tw, Ts exist (intermediate value theorem). -/
lemma inverse_exists (H : ℝ → ℝ) (Tl Tb : ℝ) (hle : Tl ≤ Tb) (hc : ContinuousOn H (Set.Icc Tl Tb)) :
    ∀ y ∈ Set.Icc (H Tl) (H Tb), ∃ T ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb, H T = y := fun y hy => by
  obtain ⟨T, hT, e⟩ := intermediate_value_Icc hle hc hy
  exact ⟨T, hT, e⟩

/-! ## Thermodynamics of dissolving NaCl(cr) (at 20 C) in the pot -/

/-- thermochemical calorie in J (Parker 1965 reports cal) -/
noncomputable def cal : ℝ := 4.184

/-- moles of NaCl for salt/water mass ratio r, in the 0.998 kg of water of 1 L at 20 C -/
noncomputable def nSalt (r : ℝ) : ℝ := 1000*0.998*r/Mnacl

/-- Enthalpy (J) of pot+solution at T, measured from pot+pure water plus crystalline NaCl at 20 C.
    Definition of the apparent molar enthalpy Ph of NaCl in the final solution: solution = (same water at T) + n*Ph(T);
    the salt started as crystal with enthalpy Hcr 20. -/
def solEnthalpy (n : ℝ) (Hw Ph Hcr : ℝ → ℝ) (T : ℝ) : ℝ := Hw T + n * (Ph T - Hcr 20)

/-- Physical premises (empirical data and definitions), see the review packet. -/
structure Premises (r : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ) : Prop where
  r_lo : 1/50 ≤ r
  r_hi : r ≤ 1/20
  hw_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Hw (Cw T) T
  hw_cap : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, 4100 ≤ Cw T
  ph_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC (r/(1+r)) T) T
  cr_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, HasDerivAt Hcr (Cpcr T) T
  cr_cap : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, 40 ≤ Cpcr T ∧ Cpcr T ≤ 100
  hinf_b : 923*cal ≤ hinf ∧ hinf ≤ 933*cal
  phiL_b : -41*cal ≤ phiL ∧ phiL ≤ 100*cal
  diss25 : Ph 25 - Hcr 25 = hinf + phiL

lemma w_range (r : ℝ) (h1 : 1/50 ≤ r) (h2 : r ≤ 1/20) : 1/51 ≤ r/(1+r) ∧ r/(1+r) ≤ 1/21 := by
  constructor
  · rw [le_div_iff₀ (by linarith)]; linarith
  · rw [div_le_iff₀ (by linarith)]; linarith

lemma n_range (r : ℝ) (h1 : 1/50 ≤ r) (h2 : r ≤ 1/20) : 0.34 ≤ nSalt r ∧ nSalt r ≤ 0.86 := by
  unfold nSalt Mnacl
  constructor
  · rw [le_div_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; linarith
  · rw [div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; linarith

theorem thermo_facts (r Tb : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ)
    (hp : Premises r Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL) (hT2 : Tb ≤ 101) :
    StrictMonoOn Hw (Set.Icc 10 Tb) ∧ StrictMonoOn (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) (Set.Icc 10 Tb) ∧
    (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) Tb, Hw x ≤ solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr x) ∧
    solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10 ≤ Hw 20 := by
  obtain ⟨hw1, hw2⟩ := w_range r hp.r_lo hp.r_hi
  obtain ⟨hn1, hn2⟩ := n_range r hp.r_lo hp.r_hi
  set w := r/(1+r)
  set n := nSalt r
  have hs1 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) Tb, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨hT.1, hT.2.trans hT2⟩
  have hs2 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨by linarith [hT.1], by linarith [hT.2]⟩
  have hs3 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 20, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨hT.1, by linarith [hT.2]⟩
  have hfl : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, -105 ≤ PhiC w T := fun T hT => phi_floor w T hw1 hT.1 hT.2
  have hng : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, PhiC w T ≤ 0 := fun T hT => (phi_neg w T hw1 hw2 hT.1 hT.2).le
  have hWs := dstrict Hw Cw 10 Tb (fun T hT => hp.hw_deriv T (hs1 T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs1 T hT)])
  have hHs : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt (solEnthalpy n Hw Ph Hcr) (Cw T + n * PhiC w T) T :=
    fun T hT => (hp.hw_deriv T hT).add (((hp.ph_deriv T hT).sub_const (Hcr 20)).const_mul n)
  have hSs := dstrict _ _ 10 Tb (fun T hT => hHs T (hs1 T hT))
    (fun T hT => by nlinarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs1 T hT), hfl T (hs1 T hT)])
  -- Kirchhoff: dissolution enthalpy Ph T - Hcr T between 20 and 25 C
  have hd : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, HasDerivAt (fun T => Ph T - Hcr T) (PhiC w T - Cpcr T) T :=
    fun T hT => (hp.ph_deriv T (hs2 T hT)).sub (hp.cr_deriv T hT)
  have hlow20 := danti _ _ 20 25 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 40 T (hd T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hng T (hs2 T hT), (hp.cr_cap T hT).1]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup20 := dmono _ _ 20 25 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 205 T (hd T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hfl T (hs2 T hT), (hp.cr_cap T hT).2]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup10 := dmono _ _ 10 20 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 105 T (hp.ph_deriv T (hs3 T hT)))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hfl T (hs3 T hT)]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup4 := dmono _ _ 10 20 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ (-4100) T (hp.hw_deriv T (hs3 T hT)))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs3 T hT)]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  simp only at hlow20 hup20 hup10 hup4
  -- Parker's numbers: dissolution enthalpy at 20 C
  obtain ⟨hi1, hi2⟩ := hp.hinf_b
  obtain ⟨hl1, hl2⟩ := hp.phiL_b
  have hcal : cal = 4.184 := rfl
  rw [hcal] at hi1 hi2 hl1 hl2
  have hd25 := hp.diss25
  refine ⟨hWs, hSs, fun x hx => ?_, ?_⟩
  · have hanti2 := danti Ph _ 10 101 hp.ph_deriv hng
    have h1 : Ph 101 ≤ Ph x := hanti2 ⟨by linarith [hx.1], by linarith [hx.2]⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by linarith [hx.2])
    have h2 := ph_drop w Ph hw1 hp.ph_deriv
    unfold solEnthalpy
    nlinarith
  · unfold solEnthalpy
    nlinarith

/-! ## Conclusions -/

/-- CONCLUSION 1 (heat capacity). For 20-50 g NaCl per kg water and 10-101 C the apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl
    in solution is negative, in (-105, 0) J/(K mol) up to the floor; dissolving the salt LOWERS the total heat capacity
    of the pot contents by n*Phi in (-91, 0) J/K (< 2.2 % of the ~4.2 kJ/K of the water). -/
theorem cs_sign : ∀ (r T : ℝ), 1/50 ≤ r → r ≤ 1/20 → 10 ≤ T → T ≤ 101 →
    -105 ≤ PhiC (r/(1+r)) T ∧ PhiC (r/(1+r)) T < 0 ∧
    -91 < nSalt r * PhiC (r/(1+r)) T ∧ nSalt r * PhiC (r/(1+r)) T < 0 := by
  intro r T h1 h2 t1 t2
  obtain ⟨w1, w2⟩ := w_range r h1 h2
  obtain ⟨n1, n2⟩ := n_range r h1 h2
  have f := phi_floor _ T w1 t1 t2
  have g := phi_neg _ T w1 w2 t1 t2
  refine ⟨f, g, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> nlinarith

/-- CONCLUSION 2 (answer). Salt added at the very start (water at 20 C) gives a boiling time no longer than salt added
    once the water has reached any later temperature Ta <= Tb. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ (r P h Tb : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr Tw Ts τw τs : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ),
    Premises r Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL → 20 ≤ Tb → Tb ≤ 101 → 0 ≤ h → h*(Tb-20) < P →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw 20) (Hw Tb), Tw H ∈ Set.Icc 10 Tb ∧ Hw (Tw H) = H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10) (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr Tb),
        Ts H ∈ Set.Icc 10 Tb ∧ solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr (Ts H) = H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw 20) (Hw Tb), HasDerivAt τw (1/(P - h*(Tw H - 20))) H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10) (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr Tb),
        HasDerivAt τs (1/(P - h*(Ts H - 20))) H) →
    ∀ Ta ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) Tb,
      boilTime τw τs Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) 20 Tb 20
        ≤ boilTime τw τs Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) 20 Tb Ta := by
  intro r P h Tb Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr Tw Ts τw τs hinf phiL hp hT1 hT2 hh hP hTw hTs hτw hτs
  obtain ⟨m1, m2, c1, c2⟩ := thermo_facts r Tb Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL hp hT2
  exact timing_core P h 20 10 Tb Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) Tw Ts τw τs hh hP (by norm_num) hT1
    m1 m2 hTw hTs hτw hτs c1 c2
checks/33a4ae57add14e699086d38537f08f5d/submitted.lean
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Deriv
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Pow.Real
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue

/-! # Salt timing for 1 L of water at 20 C: when should NaCl be added? (cycle 6)

## REVIEW PACKET
SUPPLIED BY THE USER: 1 L water at 20 C in a pot on a stove; salt will be added; goal = salted water reaches
its boiling point soonest; salt amount, pot, heater, room unspecified.

ACCEPTED EARLIER (kept): lumped Newtonian heating dH/dt = P - h (T - 20), constant stove power P, no evaporation,
salt (crystal at 20 C) dissolves instantly at the moment of addition (total enthalpy conserved).

MODEL CHOICES / ASSUMPTIONS (all appear as `Premises` fields or theorem hypotheses):
 * NaCl; r = salt mass / water mass in [1/50, 1/20] (20-50 g in the 0.998 kg of water of 1 L); mass fraction
   w = r/(1+r) (conversion proved in `w_range`), n = 1000*0.998*r/58.44 mol (`nSalt`, bounds in `n_range`).
 * Pot+water enthalpy Hw(T) has heat capacity Cw(T) >= 4100 J/K on 10..101 C (0.998 kg water >= 4.17 kJ/K; pot adds).
 * Apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl in the final solution = Laliberte (2009) correlation `PhiC`
   (empirical coefficients a1..a6 from Laliberte, J. Chem. Eng. Data 54, 1725; fit range 1.5-120 C, w <= 0.26).
   Ph(T) is the apparent molar enthalpy of NaCl in that solution: dPh/dT = PhiC (definition of Phi_C).
   Solution enthalpy = (water at T) + n*Ph(T)   (definition of apparent molar enthalpy; `solEnthalpy`).
 * Crystal NaCl enthalpy Hcr with Cp_cr in [40,100] J/K/mol on 20..25 C (Archer 1992 Part I, Eqs 4-6: Cp(298.15)=50.16;
   my evaluation of the spline gives 50.04..50.16 on 20..25 C).
 * Parker NSRDS-NBS 2 (1965): Delta H_inf(25 C, NaCl(c) -> infinite dilution) = 928 +- 5 cal/mol (printed p.26);
   Phi_L(25 C, m) (Table X, p.50) is RELATIVE to infinite dilution: Ph(25) - Hcr(25) = Hinf + Phi_L.
   For the regime m = 17.1 r in [0.34, 0.86] mol/kg Table X gives Phi_L = 83, 49, 20, -41 cal/mol at m = .2775,
   .5551, .7401, 1.1101 and dPhi_L/dm^(1/2) < 0 for m >= .185, so Phi_L in [-41, 88]; premise used: [-41, 100] cal. cal = 4.184 J.
 * Boiling temperature Tb of the final solution: 20 <= Tb <= 101 C (1 atm, <= 4.8 wt%: ideal elevation <= ~0.9 K); stove
   beats losses at Tb; Tw, Ts = temperature as function of total enthalpy (inverse of Hw, Hs; existence by IVT,
   `inverse_exists`); tau_w, tau_s = elapsed time as function of enthalpy: d tau/dH = 1/(P - h (T(H) - 20)).

PROVED (not assumed): Phi_C < 0 and >= -105 J/(K mol) for all regime/temperatures (concavity + exp bounds, `cs_sign`);
 trapezoid enclosure Ph(101) - Ph(20) >= -3363 J/mol from 11 Taylor-bounded exp evaluations (`ph_drop`);
 dissolution at 20 C >= 3890 J/mol via Kirchhoff (`thermo_facts`); salt addition at any x in [20,Tb] cools
 (Hs(x) - Hw(x) >= 0), solution enthalpy strictly increasing, mixing at 20 C stays above 10 C; and the timing
 comparison (`timing_core`, `answer_theorem`).
CONCLUSIONS: `cs_sign`, `answer_theorem` (salting at the start is no slower than salting at any later water temperature).
-/


/-! ### calculus helpers -/
lemma dmono (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, 0 ≤ f T) : MonotoneOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T (interior_subset hT)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma danti (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, f T ≤ 0) : AntitoneOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T (interior_subset hT)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma dstrict (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hf : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, 0 < f T) : StrictMonoOn F (Set.Icc a b) := by
  apply strictMonoOn_of_deriv_pos (convex_Icc a b)
  · exact fun T hT => (hF T hT).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · intro T hT; rw [(hF T (interior_subset hT)).deriv]; exact hf T (interior_subset hT)

lemma lin_deriv (F : ℝ → ℝ) (d c T : ℝ) (h : HasDerivAt F d T) :
    HasDerivAt (fun y => F y + c*y) (d + c) T :=
  (h.add ((hasDerivAt_id T).const_mul c)).congr_deriv (by simp)

/-- Taylor-polynomial upper bound for exp on [0,4] (from `Real.exp_bound`, 7 terms, applied to x/4 and raised to the 4th power) -/
noncomputable def Q (x : ℝ) : ℝ := ((∑ m ∈ Finset.range 7, (x/4)^m / (m.factorial:ℝ)) + (x/4)^7 * (8/35280))^4

lemma exp_ub (x : ℝ) (h0 : 0 ≤ x) (h4 : x ≤ 4) : Real.exp x ≤ Q x := by
  have hy : |x/4| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
  have hb := Real.exp_bound hy (n := 7) (by norm_num)
  rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ x/4)] at hb
  have h1 := (abs_le.mp hb).2
  have e : ((Nat.succ 7 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((Nat.factorial 7 : ℝ) * (7:ℕ)) = 8/35280 := by norm_num [Nat.factorial]
  rw [e] at h1
  have e2 : Real.exp x = Real.exp (x/4) ^ 4 := by
    rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
  rw [e2]; exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (Real.exp_pos _).le (by linarith) 4

/-! ### Laliberté (2009) NaCl correlation: empirical inputs (exposed) and derived consequences -/
noncomputable def Mnacl : ℝ := 58.44
noncomputable def a1 : ℝ := -0.0693559668993322
noncomputable def a2 : ℝ := -0.0782134167486952
noncomputable def a3 : ℝ := 3.84798479408635
noncomputable def a4 : ℝ := -11.2762109247072
noncomputable def a5 : ℝ := 8.73187698542672
noncomputable def a6 : ℝ := 1.81245930472755

/-- apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl in aqueous solution, J/(K mol): Mnacl * (Laliberté solute heat capacity
    Cp_i in J/(g K)); w = salt mass fraction, T in C -/
noncomputable def PhiC (w T : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  Mnacl * (a1 * Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*w) + a5 * w ^ a6)

/-- Laliberté's mixing rule Cp_sol = w_w Cp_w + w_s Cp_i gives C_solution - C_water = ms*Cp_i = (ms/M)*(M*Cp_i):
    per mole of salt the heat-capacity change is M*Cp_i = PhiC. -/
lemma apparent_capacity (ms mw M cpw cpi : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (hs : 0 < ms + mw) :
    (ms+mw) * ((mw/(ms+mw)) * cpw + (ms/(ms+mw)) * cpi) - mw * cpw = (ms/M) * (M * cpi) := by
  field_simp; ring

lemma phi_mono_w (w T : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w) : PhiC (1/51) T ≤ PhiC w T := by
  unfold PhiC
  have h1 : Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*w) ≤ Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*(1/51)) :=
    Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by unfold a4; linarith)
  have h2 : (1/51:ℝ) ^ a6 ≤ w ^ a6 := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by norm_num) h (by unfold a6; norm_num)
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have ha5 : 0 < a5 := by unfold a5; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by nlinarith) hM.le

lemma phi_neg (w T : ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w) (h2 : w ≤ 1/21) (t1 : 10 ≤ T) (t2 : T ≤ 101) : PhiC w T < 0 := by
  unfold PhiC
  have hw : 0 < w := by linarith
  have e1 : w ^ a6 ≤ w ^ ((3:ℝ)/2) :=
    Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge hw (by linarith) (by unfold a6; norm_num)
  have e2 : w ^ ((3:ℝ)/2) = w * Real.sqrt w := by
    rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, show ((3:ℝ)/2) = 1 + 1/2 by norm_num, Real.rpow_add hw, Real.rpow_one]
  have e3 : Real.sqrt w ≤ 1/4 := by rw [Real.sqrt_le_left (by norm_num)]; nlinarith
  have e4 : w ^ a6 ≤ 1/84 := by have := Real.sqrt_nonneg w; nlinarith
  have q := Real.quadratic_le_exp_of_nonneg (x := 0.01*T) (by linarith)
  set E := Real.exp (0.01*T)
  have hα : 1.2 ≤ a2*T + a3*E + a4*w := by
    unfold a2 a3 a4
    nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 101 - T) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - 10)]
  have hex : 2 ≤ Real.exp (a2*T + a3*E + a4*w) := by linarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (a2*T + a3*E + a4*w)]
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  refine mul_neg_of_pos_of_neg hM ?_
  unfold a1 a5 at *
  nlinarith

/-- Φ_C is concave in T: its value at T lies above the chord between any a < b. -/
lemma phi_chord (w a b T : ℝ) (hab : a < b) (h1 : a ≤ T) (h2 : T ≤ b) :
    (b-a) * PhiC w T ≥ (b-T) * PhiC w a + (T-a) * PhiC w b := by
  set α : ℝ → ℝ := fun s => a2*s + a3*Real.exp (0.01*s) + a4*w with hαdef
  have ha3 : 0 < a3 := by unfold a3; norm_num
  have tang : ∀ s, Real.exp (α s) ≥ Real.exp (α T) * (1 + (a2 + a3*0.01*Real.exp (0.01*T)) * (s - T)) := by
    intro s
    have e1 : Real.exp (0.01*s) ≥ Real.exp (0.01*T) * (1 + 0.01*(s-T)) := by
      have : Real.exp (0.01*s) = Real.exp (0.01*T) * Real.exp (0.01*(s-T)) := by
        rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
      rw [this]
      nlinarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (0.01*(s-T)), Real.exp_pos (0.01*T)]
    have e2 : α s - α T ≥ (a2 + a3*0.01*Real.exp (0.01*T)) * (s - T) := by
      simp only [hαdef]; nlinarith
    have e3 : Real.exp (α s) = Real.exp (α T) * Real.exp (α s - α T) := by
      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
    rw [e3]
    nlinarith [Real.add_one_le_exp (α s - α T), Real.exp_pos (α T)]
  have ta := tang a
  have tb := tang b
  have hg : (b-a) * Real.exp (α T) ≤ (b-T) * Real.exp (α a) + (T-a) * Real.exp (α b) := by
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ta (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ b - T),
               mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left tb (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - a)]
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  unfold PhiC
  have : (b-a) * (a1 * Real.exp (α T)) ≥ (b-T) * (a1 * Real.exp (α a)) + (T-a) * (a1 * Real.exp (α b)) := by
    nlinarith
  simp only [hαdef] at this
  nlinarith [mul_pos hM (by linarith : (0:ℝ) < b - a)]

/-- trapezoid lower bound: if F' = f lies above its chord on [a,b] then F b - F a ≥ (b-a)(f a + f b)/2 -/
lemma trap (F f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a < b)
    (hF : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt F (f T) T)
    (hc : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, (b-a) * f T ≥ (b-T) * f a + (T-a) * f b) :
    (b-a)*(f a + f b)/2 ≤ F b - F a := by
  have hba : 0 < b - a := by linarith
  have hd : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc a b, HasDerivAt
      (fun T => F T - F a - (f a * (T-a) + (T-a)^2 * (f b - f a) / (2*(b-a)))) (f T - ((b-T) * f a + (T-a) * f b)/(b-a)) T := by
    intro T hT
    have u : HasDerivAt (fun T : ℝ => T - a) 1 T := (hasDerivAt_id T).sub_const a
    have v := (u.const_mul (f a)).add (((u.pow 2).mul_const (f b - f a)).div_const (2*(b-a)))
    convert (hF T hT).sub_const (F a) |>.sub v using 1
    field_simp; ring
  have := dmono _ _ a b hd (fun T hT => by
    have := hc T hT
    rw [sub_nonneg, div_le_iff₀ hba]; linarith) ⟨le_rfl, hab.le⟩ ⟨hab.le, le_rfl⟩ hab.le
  simp only at this
  have e : (f a * (b-a) + (b-a)^2 * (f b - f a) / (2*(b-a))) = (b-a)*(f a + f b)/2 := by
    field_simp; ring
  simp at this
  nlinarith [e]

lemma node (w T V β U L : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w)
    (hh : 0 ≤ T ∧ T ≤ 400 ∧ Q (0.01*T) ≤ V ∧ a2*T + a3*V + a4*(1/51) ≤ β ∧ 0 ≤ β ∧ β ≤ 4 ∧ Q β ≤ U ∧
      -L ≤ Mnacl*a1*U) : -L ≤ PhiC w T := by
  obtain ⟨h0, h4, hV, hb, hb0, hb4, hU, hL⟩ := hh
  refine le_trans ?_ (phi_mono_w w T h)
  have e1 : Real.exp (0.01*T) ≤ V := (exp_ub _ (by linarith) (by linarith)).trans hV
  have ha3 : 0 < a3 := by unfold a3; norm_num
  have e2 : Real.exp (a2*T + a3*Real.exp (0.01*T) + a4*(1/51)) ≤ U :=
    (Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e1 ha3.le])).trans ((exp_ub β hb0 hb4).trans hU)
  have h5 : 0 ≤ a5 * (1/51:ℝ)^a6 := mul_nonneg (by unfold a5; norm_num) (Real.rpow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
  have ha1 : a1 < 0 := by unfold a1; norm_num
  have hM : 0 < Mnacl := by unfold Mnacl; norm_num
  unfold PhiC
  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e2 (mul_pos hM (neg_pos.mpr ha1)).le, mul_nonneg hM.le h5]

lemma nodes (w : ℝ) (h : 1/51 ≤ w) : -(104.51:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 10 ∧ -(74.77:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 20 ∧ -(56.07:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 30 ∧ -(44.29:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 40 ∧ -(37.06:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 50 ∧ -(33.04:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 60 ∧ -(31.6:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 70 ∧ -(32.65:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 80 ∧ -(36.75:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 90 ∧ -(45.49:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 100 ∧ -(46.73:ℝ) ≤ PhiC w 101 :=
  ⟨node w 10 1.10519 3.2497 25.784 104.51 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 20 1.22142 2.9148 18.447 74.77 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 30 1.34987 2.6269 13.832 56.07 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 40 1.49184 2.3911 10.927 44.29 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 50 1.64874 2.2127 9.142 37.06 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 60 1.82213 2.0978 8.15 33.04 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 70 2.01377 2.0531 7.794 31.6 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 80 2.22556 2.0859 8.053 32.65 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 90 2.45962 2.2044 9.066 36.75 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 100 2.7183 2.4177 11.222 45.49 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1]),
   node w 101 2.74562 2.4446 11.527 46.73 h (by norm_num [Q, Finset.sum_range_succ, Nat.factorial, a2, a3, a4, Mnacl, a1])⟩

lemma piece (w : ℝ) (Ph : ℝ → ℝ) (a b La Lb : ℝ) (hab : a < b) (ha : 10 ≤ a) (hb : b ≤ 101)
    (hPh : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC w T) T)
    (hA : -La ≤ PhiC w a) (hB : -Lb ≤ PhiC w b) : -((b-a)*(La+Lb)/2) ≤ Ph b - Ph a := by
  have := trap Ph (PhiC w) a b hab (fun T hT => hPh T ⟨by linarith [hT.1], by linarith [hT.2]⟩)
    (fun T hT => phi_chord w a b T hab hT.1 hT.2)
  nlinarith

lemma phi_floor (w T : ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w) (t1 : 10 ≤ T) (t2 : T ≤ 101) : -105 ≤ PhiC w T := by
  have c := phi_chord w 10 101 T (by norm_num) t1 t2
  obtain ⟨n10, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, n101⟩ := nodes w h1
  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 101 - T) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 105 - 104.51),
             mul_nonneg (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ T - 10) (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 46.73 - 0)]

/-- Total change of apparent molar enthalpy of NaCl from 20 C to 101 C is at least -3363 J/mol. -/
lemma ph_drop (w : ℝ) (Ph : ℝ → ℝ) (h1 : 1/51 ≤ w)
    (hPh : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC w T) T) : -3363 ≤ Ph 101 - Ph 20 := by
  obtain ⟨n10, n20, n30, n40, n50, n60, n70, n80, n90, n100, n101⟩ := nodes w h1
  have p0 := piece w Ph 20 30 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n20 n30
  have p1 := piece w Ph 30 40 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n30 n40
  have p2 := piece w Ph 40 50 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n40 n50
  have p3 := piece w Ph 50 60 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n50 n60
  have p4 := piece w Ph 60 70 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n60 n70
  have p5 := piece w Ph 70 80 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n70 n80
  have p6 := piece w Ph 80 90 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n80 n90
  have p7 := piece w Ph 90 100 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n90 n100
  have p8 := piece w Ph 100 101 _ _ (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hPh n100 n101
  norm_num at *
  linarith

/-! ## Heating-time model (accepted Newtonian lumped model; state variable = total enthalpy H) -/

/-- Time to reach the boiling point when salt is added while the pure water is at Ta: pure-water branch up to
    total enthalpy Hw Ta, then solution branch from the same enthalpy up to Hs Tb.
    τw, τs = elapsed time as a function of total enthalpy on the two branches. -/
def boilTime (τw τs Hw Hs : ℝ → ℝ) (T0 Tb Ta : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (τw (Hw Ta) - τw (Hw T0)) + (τs (Hs Tb) - τs (Hw Ta))

theorem timing_core (P h Tr Tl Tb : ℝ) (Hw Hs Tw Ts τw τs : ℝ → ℝ)
    (hh : 0 ≤ h) (hP : h*(Tb-Tr) < P) (hl : Tl ≤ Tr) (hr : Tr ≤ Tb)
    (hHw : StrictMonoOn Hw (Set.Icc Tl Tb)) (hHs : StrictMonoOn Hs (Set.Icc Tl Tb))
    (hTw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Tb), Tw H ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb ∧ Hw (Tw H) = H)
    (hTs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hs Tl) (Hs Tb), Ts H ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb ∧ Hs (Ts H) = H)
    (hτw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Tb), HasDerivAt τw (1/(P - h*(Tw H - Tr))) H)
    (hτs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hs Tl) (Hs Tb), HasDerivAt τs (1/(P - h*(Ts H - Tr))) H)
    (hcool : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc Tr Tb, Hw x ≤ Hs x) (hlow : Hs Tl ≤ Hw Tr) :
    ∀ Ta ∈ Set.Icc Tr Tb, boilTime τw τs Hw Hs Tr Tb Tr ≤ boilTime τw τs Hw Hs Tr Tb Ta := by
  intro Ta hTa
  have hTrI : Tr ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl, hr⟩
  have hTaI : Ta ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl.trans hTa.1, hTa.2⟩
  have hTbI : Tb ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb := ⟨hl.trans hr, le_rfl⟩
  have w1 : Hw Tr ≤ Hw Ta := hHw.monotoneOn hTrI hTaI hTa.1
  have w2 : Hw Ta ≤ Hw Tb := hHw.monotoneOn hTaI hTbI hTa.2
  have s1 : Hw Tb ≤ Hs Tb := hcool Tb ⟨hr, le_rfl⟩
  have hpos : ∀ x, x ≤ Tb → 0 < P - h*(x - Tr) := by
    intro x hx
    have : h*(x-Tr) ≤ h*(Tb-Tr) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hh
    linarith
  have key : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw Tr) (Hw Ta), 1/(P - h*(Tw H - Tr)) - 1/(P - h*(Ts H - Tr)) ≥ 0 := by
    intro H hH
    obtain ⟨hx, hxH⟩ := hTw H ⟨hH.1, hH.2.trans w2⟩
    obtain ⟨hy, hyH⟩ := hTs H ⟨hlow.trans hH.1, hH.2.trans (w2.trans s1)⟩
    have hxr : Tr ≤ Tw H := by
      by_contra hc
      have := hHw hx hTrI (not_le.mp hc)
      rw [hxH] at this
      linarith [hH.1]
    have hc := hcool _ ⟨hxr, hx.2⟩
    rw [hxH] at hc
    have hyx : Ts H ≤ Tw H := by
      by_contra hcon
      have := hHs hx hy (not_le.mp hcon)
      rw [hyH] at this
      linarith
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith : Ts H - Tr ≤ Tw H - Tr) hh
    have := one_div_le_one_div_of_le (hpos _ hx.2) (by linarith : P - h*(Tw H - Tr) ≤ P - h*(Ts H - Tr))
    linarith
  have mono := dmono (fun H => τw H - τs H) _ (Hw Tr) (Hw Ta)
    (fun H hH => (hτw H ⟨hH.1, hH.2.trans w2⟩).sub (hτs H ⟨hlow.trans hH.1, hH.2.trans (w2.trans s1)⟩)) key
  have := mono ⟨le_rfl, w1⟩ ⟨w1, le_rfl⟩ w1
  simp only [boilTime] at this ⊢
  linarith

/-- sanity: the inverse temperature functions Tw, Ts exist (intermediate value theorem). -/
lemma inverse_exists (H : ℝ → ℝ) (Tl Tb : ℝ) (hle : Tl ≤ Tb) (hc : ContinuousOn H (Set.Icc Tl Tb)) :
    ∀ y ∈ Set.Icc (H Tl) (H Tb), ∃ T ∈ Set.Icc Tl Tb, H T = y := fun y hy => by
  obtain ⟨T, hT, e⟩ := intermediate_value_Icc hle hc hy
  exact ⟨T, hT, e⟩

/-! ## Thermodynamics of dissolving NaCl(cr) (at 20 C) in the pot -/

/-- thermochemical calorie in J (Parker 1965 reports cal) -/
noncomputable def cal : ℝ := 4.184

/-- moles of NaCl for salt/water mass ratio r, in the 0.998 kg of water of 1 L at 20 C -/
noncomputable def nSalt (r : ℝ) : ℝ := 1000*0.998*r/Mnacl

/-- Enthalpy (J) of pot+solution at T, measured from pot+pure water plus crystalline NaCl at 20 C.
    Definition of the apparent molar enthalpy Ph of NaCl in the final solution: solution = (same water at T) + n*Ph(T);
    the salt started as crystal with enthalpy Hcr 20. -/
def solEnthalpy (n : ℝ) (Hw Ph Hcr : ℝ → ℝ) (T : ℝ) : ℝ := Hw T + n * (Ph T - Hcr 20)

/-- Physical premises (empirical data and definitions), see the review packet. -/
structure Premises (r : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ) : Prop where
  r_lo : 1/50 ≤ r
  r_hi : r ≤ 1/20
  hw_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Hw (Cw T) T
  hw_cap : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, 4100 ≤ Cw T
  ph_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt Ph (PhiC (r/(1+r)) T) T
  cr_deriv : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, HasDerivAt Hcr (Cpcr T) T
  cr_cap : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, 40 ≤ Cpcr T ∧ Cpcr T ≤ 100
  hinf_b : 923*cal ≤ hinf ∧ hinf ≤ 933*cal
  phiL_b : -41*cal ≤ phiL ∧ phiL ≤ 100*cal
  diss25 : Ph 25 - Hcr 25 = hinf + phiL

lemma w_range (r : ℝ) (h1 : 1/50 ≤ r) (h2 : r ≤ 1/20) : 1/51 ≤ r/(1+r) ∧ r/(1+r) ≤ 1/21 := by
  constructor
  · rw [le_div_iff₀ (by linarith)]; linarith
  · rw [div_le_iff₀ (by linarith)]; linarith

lemma n_range (r : ℝ) (h1 : 1/50 ≤ r) (h2 : r ≤ 1/20) : 0.34 ≤ nSalt r ∧ nSalt r ≤ 0.86 := by
  unfold nSalt Mnacl
  constructor
  · rw [le_div_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; linarith
  · rw [div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; linarith

theorem thermo_facts (r Tb : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ)
    (hp : Premises r Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL) (hT2 : Tb ≤ 101) :
    StrictMonoOn Hw (Set.Icc 10 Tb) ∧ StrictMonoOn (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) (Set.Icc 10 Tb) ∧
    (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) Tb, Hw x ≤ solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr x) ∧
    solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10 ≤ Hw 20 := by
  obtain ⟨hw1, hw2⟩ := w_range r hp.r_lo hp.r_hi
  obtain ⟨hn1, hn2⟩ := n_range r hp.r_lo hp.r_hi
  set w := r/(1+r)
  set n := nSalt r
  have hs1 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) Tb, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨hT.1, hT.2.trans hT2⟩
  have hs2 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨by linarith [hT.1], by linarith [hT.2]⟩
  have hs3 : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 20, T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101 := fun T hT => ⟨hT.1, by linarith [hT.2]⟩
  have hfl : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, -105 ≤ PhiC w T := fun T hT => phi_floor w T hw1 hT.1 hT.2
  have hng : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, PhiC w T ≤ 0 := fun T hT => (phi_neg w T hw1 hw2 hT.1 hT.2).le
  have hWs := dstrict Hw Cw 10 Tb (fun T hT => hp.hw_deriv T (hs1 T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs1 T hT)])
  have hHs : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (10:ℝ) 101, HasDerivAt (solEnthalpy n Hw Ph Hcr) (Cw T + n * PhiC w T) T :=
    fun T hT => (hp.hw_deriv T hT).add (((hp.ph_deriv T hT).sub_const (Hcr 20)).const_mul n)
  have hSs := dstrict _ _ 10 Tb (fun T hT => hHs T (hs1 T hT))
    (fun T hT => by nlinarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs1 T hT), hfl T (hs1 T hT)])
  -- Kirchhoff: dissolution enthalpy Ph T - Hcr T between 20 and 25 C
  have hd : ∀ T ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) 25, HasDerivAt (fun T => Ph T - Hcr T) (PhiC w T - Cpcr T) T :=
    fun T hT => (hp.ph_deriv T (hs2 T hT)).sub (hp.cr_deriv T hT)
  have hlow20 := danti _ _ 20 25 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 40 T (hd T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hng T (hs2 T hT), (hp.cr_cap T hT).1]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup20 := dmono _ _ 20 25 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 205 T (hd T hT))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hfl T (hs2 T hT), (hp.cr_cap T hT).2]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup10 := dmono _ _ 10 20 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ 105 T (hp.ph_deriv T (hs3 T hT)))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hfl T (hs3 T hT)]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  have hup4 := dmono _ _ 10 20 (fun T hT => lin_deriv _ _ (-4100) T (hp.hw_deriv T (hs3 T hT)))
    (fun T hT => by linarith [hp.hw_cap T (hs3 T hT)]) ⟨le_rfl, by norm_num⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by norm_num)
  simp only at hlow20 hup20 hup10 hup4
  -- Parker's numbers: dissolution enthalpy at 20 C
  obtain ⟨hi1, hi2⟩ := hp.hinf_b
  obtain ⟨hl1, hl2⟩ := hp.phiL_b
  have hcal : cal = 4.184 := rfl
  rw [hcal] at hi1 hi2 hl1 hl2
  have hd25 := hp.diss25
  refine ⟨hWs, hSs, fun x hx => ?_, ?_⟩
  · have hanti2 := danti Ph _ 10 101 hp.ph_deriv hng
    have h1 : Ph 101 ≤ Ph x := hanti2 ⟨by linarith [hx.1], by linarith [hx.2]⟩ ⟨by norm_num, le_rfl⟩ (by linarith [hx.2])
    have h2 := ph_drop w Ph hw1 hp.ph_deriv
    unfold solEnthalpy
    nlinarith
  · unfold solEnthalpy
    nlinarith

/-! ## Conclusions -/

/-- CONCLUSION 1 (heat capacity). For 20-50 g NaCl per kg water and 10-101 C the apparent molar heat capacity of NaCl
    in solution is negative, in (-105, 0) J/(K mol) up to the floor; dissolving the salt LOWERS the total heat capacity
    of the pot contents by n*Phi in (-91, 0) J/K (< 2.2 % of the ~4.2 kJ/K of the water). -/
theorem cs_sign : ∀ (r T : ℝ), 1/50 ≤ r → r ≤ 1/20 → 10 ≤ T → T ≤ 101 →
    -105 ≤ PhiC (r/(1+r)) T ∧ PhiC (r/(1+r)) T < 0 ∧
    -91 < nSalt r * PhiC (r/(1+r)) T ∧ nSalt r * PhiC (r/(1+r)) T < 0 := by
  intro r T h1 h2 t1 t2
  obtain ⟨w1, w2⟩ := w_range r h1 h2
  obtain ⟨n1, n2⟩ := n_range r h1 h2
  have f := phi_floor _ T w1 t1 t2
  have g := phi_neg _ T w1 w2 t1 t2
  refine ⟨f, g, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> nlinarith

/-- CONCLUSION 2 (answer). Salt added at the very start (water at 20 C) gives a boiling time no longer than salt added
    once the water has reached any later temperature Ta <= Tb. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ (r P h Tb : ℝ) (Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr Tw Ts τw τs : ℝ → ℝ) (hinf phiL : ℝ),
    Premises r Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL → 20 ≤ Tb → Tb ≤ 101 → 0 ≤ h → h*(Tb-20) < P →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw 20) (Hw Tb), Tw H ∈ Set.Icc 10 Tb ∧ Hw (Tw H) = H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10) (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr Tb),
        Ts H ∈ Set.Icc 10 Tb ∧ solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr (Ts H) = H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Hw 20) (Hw Tb), HasDerivAt τw (1/(P - h*(Tw H - 20))) H) →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr 10) (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr Tb),
        HasDerivAt τs (1/(P - h*(Ts H - 20))) H) →
    ∀ Ta ∈ Set.Icc (20:ℝ) Tb,
      boilTime τw τs Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) 20 Tb 20
        ≤ boilTime τw τs Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) 20 Tb Ta := by
  intro r P h Tb Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr Tw Ts τw τs hinf phiL hp hT1 hT2 hh hP hTw hTs hτw hτs
  obtain ⟨m1, m2, c1, c2⟩ := thermo_facts r Tb Cw Hw Ph Hcr Cpcr hinf phiL hp hT2
  exact timing_core P h 20 10 Tb Hw (solEnthalpy (nSalt r) Hw Ph Hcr) Tw Ts τw τs hh hP (by norm_num) hT1
    m1 m2 hTw hTs hτw hτs c1 c2
checks/dcd7a1b461ab4ea1832bd51aed75b96c/submitted.lean
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Deriv
import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue

/-! Model (see report.txt).  H = enthalpy (J) measured from "everything at room temperature Tr".
 Pure water, heat capacity Cw: temperature Tr + H/Cw.
 Salt solution, total heat capacity Cw+Cs>0 (Cs of EITHER sign; literature: Cs<0), dissolution enthalpy D (at Tr) of either sign:
 temperature Tr + (H-D)/(Cw+Cs).  Salt (at Tr) is mixed in instantly, so H is conserved.
 Heater power P constant, loss h*(T-Tr):  dH/dt = P - h*(T-Tr).
 Separating variables, the time to move from H1 to H2 is Fw/Fs(H2) - Fw/Fs(H1) where
 Fw' = 1/(P - h*(H/Cw)), Fs' = 1/(P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs))) (explicit logarithms below).
 Tb = boiling point of the final solution (fixed by salt amount, not by timing). -/

/-- time-integral of dH/(P - loss) for pure water (antiderivative, h>0) -/
noncomputable def Fw (Cw P h H : ℝ) : ℝ := -(Cw/h) * Real.log (P - h*(H/Cw))
/-- same for the salt solution -/
noncomputable def Fs (Cw Cs D P h H : ℝ) : ℝ := -((Cw+Cs)/h) * Real.log (P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs)))

/-- Total time to reach Tb from T0 when the salt is added once the water is at Ta:
 heating water alone from T0 to Ta, then solution from the post-mixing state up to Tb. -/
noncomputable def boilTime (Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb Ta : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (Fw Cw P h (Cw*(Ta-Tr)) - Fw Cw P h (Cw*(T0-Tr))) +
  (Fs Cw Cs D P h ((Cw+Cs)*(Tb-Tr)+D) - Fs Cw Cs D P h (Cw*(Ta-Tr)))

lemma G_deriv (Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb : ℝ)
    (hCw : 0 < Cw) (hC : 0 < Cw + Cs) (hh : 0 < h)
    (hP : h*(Tb-Tr) < P)
    (hs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs))) :
    ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), HasDerivAt (fun H => Fw Cw P h H - Fs Cw Cs D P h H)
      (1/(P - h*(H/Cw)) - 1/(P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs)))) H := by
  have hw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*(H/Cw) := by
    intro H hH
    have : H/Cw ≤ Tb - Tr := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hCw]; nlinarith [hH.2]
    nlinarith
  intro H hH
  have a1 : HasDerivAt (fun H : ℝ => P - h*(H/Cw)) (-(h/Cw)) H := by
    have := ((hasDerivAt_id H).div_const Cw).const_mul h
    simpa [div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm] using (this.const_sub P)
  have a2 : HasDerivAt (fun H : ℝ => P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs))) (-(h/(Cw+Cs))) H := by
    have := (((hasDerivAt_id H).sub_const D).div_const (Cw+Cs)).const_mul h
    simpa [div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm] using (this.const_sub P)
  have l1 := (a1.log (hw H hH).ne').const_mul (-(Cw/h))
  have l2 := (a2.log (hs H hH).ne').const_mul (-((Cw+Cs)/h))
  have := l1.sub l2
  convert this using 1
  · funext x; rfl
  · have e1 := (hw H hH).ne'
    have e2 := (hs H hH).ne'
    have e3 := hh.ne'
    have e4 := hCw.ne'
    have e5 := hC.ne'
    field_simp

/-- Salt added at water temperature x changes the temperature at once by
 (D + Cs (x-Tr))/(Cw+Cs) downward (a cooling if D + Cs (x-Tr) > 0).  -/
lemma mix_iff (Cw Cs D Tr x : ℝ) (hCw : 0 < Cw) (hC : 0 < Cw + Cs) :
    (Cw*(x-Tr) - D)/(Cw+Cs) ≤ Cw*(x-Tr)/Cw ↔ 0 ≤ D + Cs*(x-Tr) := by
  rw [mul_div_cancel_left₀ _ hCw.ne', div_le_iff₀ hC]
  constructor <;> intro h <;> nlinarith

lemma G_mono (Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb : ℝ)
    (hCw : 0 < Cw) (hC : 0 < Cw + Cs) (hh : 0 < h)
    (hP : h*(Tb-Tr) < P)
    (hs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs)))
    (S : Set ℝ) (hS : S ⊆ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr))) (hconv : Convex ℝ S)
    (sgn : ∀ H ∈ S, (H-D)/(Cw+Cs) ≤ H/Cw) :
    MonotoneOn (fun H => Fw Cw P h H - Fs Cw Cs D P h H) S := by
  have hw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*(H/Cw) := by
    intro H hH
    have : H/Cw ≤ Tb - Tr := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hCw]; nlinarith [hH.2]
    nlinarith
  have hd := G_deriv Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb hCw hC hh hP hs
  apply monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg hconv
  · intro H hH; exact (hd H (hS hH)).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · intro H hH; exact (hd H (hS (interior_subset hH))).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro H hH
    have hH' := interior_subset hH
    rw [(hd H (hS hH')).deriv]
    apply sub_nonneg.mpr
    apply one_div_le_one_div_of_le (hw H (hS hH'))
    have := sgn H hH'
    nlinarith

lemma G_anti (Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb : ℝ)
    (hCw : 0 < Cw) (hC : 0 < Cw + Cs) (hh : 0 < h)
    (hP : h*(Tb-Tr) < P)
    (hs : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs)))
    (S : Set ℝ) (hS : S ⊆ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr))) (hconv : Convex ℝ S)
    (sgn : ∀ H ∈ S, H/Cw ≤ (H-D)/(Cw+Cs)) :
    AntitoneOn (fun H => Fw Cw P h H - Fs Cw Cs D P h H) S := by
  have hw : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*(H/Cw) := by
    intro H hH
    have : H/Cw ≤ Tb - Tr := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hCw]; nlinarith [hH.2]
    nlinarith
  have hd := G_deriv Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb hCw hC hh hP hs
  apply antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos hconv
  · intro H hH; exact (hd H (hS hH)).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt
  · intro H hH; exact (hd H (hS (interior_subset hH))).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  · intro H hH
    have hH' := interior_subset hH
    rw [(hd H (hS hH')).deriv]
    apply sub_nonpos.mpr
    apply one_div_le_one_div_of_le (hs H (hS hH'))
    have := sgn H hH'
    nlinarith

/-- Comparison lemma (sign-free; proved, used by the answer below).  Salt of fixed amount (Cs, D arbitrary sign, C=Cw+Cs>0).
 If between water temperatures Ta1 ≤ Ta2 salt addition always COOLS (D+Cs(x-Tr) ≥ 0 for x in [Ta1,Ta2]),
 adding at Ta1 is no slower than at Ta2; if it always WARMS (≤ 0), adding at Ta2 is no slower. -/
theorem compare_theorem : ∀ (Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb : ℝ),
    0 < Cw → 0 < Cw + Cs → 0 < h → h*(Tb-Tr) < P → Tr ≤ T0 →
    (∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*((H-D)/(Cw+Cs))) →
    ∀ Ta1 Ta2 : ℝ, T0 ≤ Ta1 → Ta1 ≤ Ta2 → Ta2 ≤ Tb →
      ((∀ x, Ta1 ≤ x → x ≤ Ta2 → 0 ≤ D + Cs*(x-Tr)) →
          boilTime Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb Ta1 ≤ boilTime Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb Ta2) ∧
      ((∀ x, Ta1 ≤ x → x ≤ Ta2 → D + Cs*(x-Tr) ≤ 0) →
          boilTime Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb Ta2 ≤ boilTime Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb Ta1) := by
  intro Cw Cs D P h Tr T0 Tb hCw hC hh hP hTr hs Ta1 Ta2 h1 h12 h2
  have hmem : ∀ x, Ta1 ≤ x → x ≤ Ta2 → Cw*(x-Tr) ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)) := fun x a b =>
    ⟨by nlinarith, by nlinarith⟩
  have hS : Set.Icc (Cw*(Ta1-Tr)) (Cw*(Ta2-Tr)) ⊆ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)) := by
    intro H hH
    exact ⟨by nlinarith [hH.1], by nlinarith [hH.2]⟩
  have hHx : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc (Cw*(Ta1-Tr)) (Cw*(Ta2-Tr)), ∃ x, Ta1 ≤ x ∧ x ≤ Ta2 ∧ H = Cw*(x-Tr) := by
    intro H hH
    refine ⟨H/Cw + Tr, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · have : Ta1 - Tr ≤ H/Cw := by rw [le_div_iff₀ hCw]; nlinarith [hH.1]
      linarith
    · have : H/Cw ≤ Ta2 - Tr := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hCw]; nlinarith [hH.2]
      linarith
    · field_simp; ring
  constructor
  · intro hx
    have m := G_mono Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb hCw hC hh hP hs _ hS (convex_Icc _ _) (by
      intro H hH
      obtain ⟨x, a, b, rfl⟩ := hHx H hH
      exact (mix_iff Cw Cs D Tr x hCw hC).mpr (hx x a b))
      ⟨le_rfl, by nlinarith⟩ ⟨by nlinarith, le_rfl⟩ (by nlinarith)
    simp only [boilTime]; simp only at m; linarith
  · intro hx
    have m := G_anti Cw Cs D P h Tr Tb hCw hC hh hP hs _ hS (convex_Icc _ _) (by
      intro H hH
      obtain ⟨x, a, b, rfl⟩ := hHx H hH
      have := hx x a b
      have h3 : ¬ ((Cw*(x-Tr) - D)/(Cw+Cs) < Cw*(x-Tr)/Cw) → True := fun _ => trivial
      rw [mul_div_cancel_left₀ _ hCw.ne', le_div_iff₀ hC]
      nlinarith)
      ⟨le_rfl, by nlinarith⟩ ⟨by nlinarith, le_rfl⟩ (by nlinarith)
    simp only [boilTime]; simp only at m; linarith


/-! ## Salt-specific part (NaCl, 1 L of water).  Inputs are labelled; nothing numerical below is a measurement.

 Choices.  Salt = NaCl, molar mass M = 58.44 g/mol.  1 L of water at 20 C has mass mw = 0.998 kg
 (density 0.9982 kg/L).  The salt/water ratio r = (salt mass)/(water mass) (NOT the mass fraction of the
 final solution; the fraction is w = r/(1+r), proved in `cs_theorem`).  Regime: 0.02 <= r <= 0.05,
 i.e. about 20-50 g NaCl in the litre (2-4.8 wt%; seawater-like to very salty cooking water).
 Cs is DERIVED, not fitted: by definition of the apparent molar heat capacity Phi of the salt in solution,
 (total heat capacity of solution) = (heat capacity of the water) + n*Phi, so Cs = n*Phi, n = moles of salt.
 Phi is temperature dependent; the lumped model needs one constant Cs, so Phi below is the SECANT MEAN of
 Phi(T,m) over 20 C..Tb, which makes the linear mixing-enthalpy D + Cs (T-Tr) exact at both ends 20 C and Tb.
 Empirical inputs (EXPOSED PREMISES, from the Laliberte (2009) NaCl correlation, fit range 1.5-120 C,
 w <= 0.26, evaluated numerically outside Lean -- a synthetic calculation from a published correlation):
   * secant-mean Phi in [-41, -27] J/K/mol for r in [0.02, 0.05] (computed: -40.7 at r=0.02 ... -27.9 at 0.05);
   * enthalpy of solution of NaCl(s) at ~20-25 C, hsol >= 3800 J/mol (NBS value +3.88 kJ/mol, endothermic);
   * boiling point of the solution Tb in [100, 101] C (elevation <= ~1 K for these amounts);
   * pot+water heat capacity Cw >= 4170 J/K (at least the 0.998 kg of water, 4.18 J/g/K).
 D = n*hsol is the enthalpy absorbed when the salt (at room temperature Tr) dissolves at Tr. -/

/-- Cs = n*Phi: sign, size, and the ratio conversion. -/
theorem cs_theorem : ∀ (r M mw n Phi ms mwater : ℝ),
    0.02 ≤ r → r ≤ 0.05 → M = 58.44 → mw = 0.998 → n = 1000*mw*r/M →
    -41 ≤ Phi → Phi ≤ -27 → 0 < ms → 0 < mwater →
    (n*Phi < 0 ∧ -36 ≤ n*Phi) ∧ ms/(ms+mwater) = (ms/mwater)/(1+ms/mwater) := by
  intro r M mw n Phi ms mwater hr1 hr2 hM hmw hn hP1 hP2 hms hmwa
  have hn0 : 0 < n := by rw [hn, hM, hmw]; positivity
  have hn1 : n ≤ 0.8540 := by
    rw [hn, hM, hmw, div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; nlinarith
  refine ⟨⟨by nlinarith, by nlinarith⟩, ?_⟩
  field_simp

/-- ANSWER.  Accepted model (lumped heat capacity Cw, constant stove power P, Newton loss h, no evaporation,
 salt at room temperature Tr = T0 = 20 C dissolves instantly, enthalpy conserved).  Under the premises above
 (r in [0.02,0.05], Cs = n*Phi, D = n*hsol, Tb in [100,101], stove stronger than losses at Tb), adding the
 salt right at the start (Ta = T0) is no slower than adding it at any later water temperature Ta in [T0,Tb].
 The condition D + Cs (x-Tr) >= 0 (salt addition cools / does not warm) is CHECKED here, not assumed, and
 the 'stove beats losses' condition for the salt solution is derived from it. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ (Cw P h Tr T0 Tb r M mw n Phi hsol : ℝ),
    Tr = 20 → T0 = 20 → 100 ≤ Tb → Tb ≤ 101 → 4170 ≤ Cw → 0 < h → h*(Tb-Tr) < P →
    0.02 ≤ r → r ≤ 0.05 → M = 58.44 → mw = 0.998 → n = 1000*mw*r/M →
    -41 ≤ Phi → Phi ≤ -27 → 3800 ≤ hsol →
    ∀ Ta : ℝ, T0 ≤ Ta → Ta ≤ Tb →
      boilTime Cw (n*Phi) (n*hsol) P h Tr T0 Tb T0 ≤ boilTime Cw (n*Phi) (n*hsol) P h Tr T0 Tb Ta := by
  intro Cw P h Tr T0 Tb r M mw n Phi hsol hTr hT0 hb1 hb2 hCw hh hP hr1 hr2 hM hmw hn hP1 hP2 hs Ta h1 h2
  have hn0 : 0 < n := by rw [hn, hM, hmw]; positivity
  have hn1 : n ≤ 0.8540 := by
    rw [hn, hM, hmw, div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num)]; nlinarith
  have hCs : n*Phi < 0 := by nlinarith
  have hCs2 : -36 ≤ n*Phi := by nlinarith
  have hCw0 : 0 < Cw := by linarith
  have hC : 0 < Cw + n*Phi := by linarith
  have hd : 0 ≤ Tb - Tr := by linarith
  have hd2 : Tb - Tr ≤ 81 := by linarith
  -- enthalpy of solution at the boiling point is still >= 0 (checked)
  have hE0 : 0 ≤ hsol + Phi*(Tb-Tr) := by nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ Phi + 41) hd]
  have hE : 0 ≤ n*hsol + n*Phi*(Tb-Tr) := by nlinarith [mul_nonneg hn0.le hE0]
  have hstove : ∀ H ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Cw*(Tb-Tr)), 0 < P - h*((H-n*hsol)/(Cw+n*Phi)) := by
    intro H hH
    have : (H-n*hsol)/(Cw+n*Phi) ≤ Tb - Tr := by
      rw [div_le_iff₀ hC]; nlinarith [hH.2]
    nlinarith
  refine (compare_theorem Cw (n*Phi) (n*hsol) P h Tr T0 Tb hCw0 hC hh hP (by linarith) hstove
    T0 Ta le_rfl h1 h2).1 ?_
  intro x hx1 hx2
  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ Tb - x) (by linarith : 0 ≤ -(n*Phi))]

accepted
